🟢 **PA-1 APPLIED 2026-09-18 — PENNSYLVANIA HAS LEGISLATIVE GEOGRAPHY FOR THE FIRST TIME.** 253 boundaries and 253 districts — 203 House + 50 Senate — 0 errors, `districts` 8,724 → 8,977 and `geofence_boundaries` 62,807 → 63,060, both moving by **exactly 253**. 🔴🔴 **A COUNT CAN NEVER DATE A PENNSYLVANIA MAP**: Pa. Const. Art. II §16 FIXES the chambers at 203 and 50, so that shape fits the 2012 plan, the 2022 plan and every future one — MN-1 had the weaker form of this and settled it on one renumbered district. **This wave settled it on all 253**, testing every TIGER polygon at its own internal point against **PennDOT’s own layers** (PASDA — a Commonwealth boundary set, not a Census mirror): **203/203 and 50/50 agree, 0 differ**. ⚠ **And the sweep was then made to fail** — the same comparison against the TIGER 2018 polygons differs on 44 House and 5 Senate districts, so the verifier can tell the maps apart (`scripts/verify-pa-tiger-vintage.mjs`). NC-3 met one instance of this; in PA it is the rule. **The key is (mtfcc, geo_id).** ⚠ **STAGE 1 OWED NO PLACE LAYER** — PA’s 1,013 G4110, 989 G4210 and 2,573 G4040 rows were loaded HOURS EARLIER by the Civic Spaces municipal wave (#541), so Philadelphia `4260000` and State College `4273808` already had geometry. **Measure the layer before loading it.** 🟢 **`check:child-county` is red for exactly MI 533 + PA 1,013 + OH 925 = 2,471 — that wave’s debt, not this one’s**: the check defines a child as G4110/G5420/G5400/G5410, so G5210/G5220 cannot appear, which is why no refresh was needed (read in the check’s source, not assumed). ✅ **Both pre-flight gates watched failing first**, per-district control **50/50 and 203/203 resolve to exactly one**, Ohio negative control 0, six anchors agree with PennDOT in production, idempotent on re-run, `offices_missing_terms` **unmoved at 823/655**, `check:reachability` nothing regressed with two buckets below baseline. 🟢 Philadelphia city `4260000` measures **142.422 sq mi against the county’s 142.422** — coterminous, so the government keys on the TIGER place, the Columbus/Macon rule unchanged. ▶ **Next: stage 2 — 253 seats, the largest single legislature block the program will ever seat.** Full record: [`pa.md`](./pa.md).

Rabb”, already in production as a PA-3 candidate **with a photo**). A diminutive defeats a pair guard exactly as MN-2's Steve/Steven did. an empty connection string looks exactly like a hung database.** Source the environment in the same command that uses it. ✅ **END TO END**: Philadelphia City Hall returns all 7 row officers; State College returns all 13 county offices; 🟢 **PA-5a APPLIED 2026-09-19 — THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY HAS PORTRAITS, 252 OF 253.** The sheet Cantrell approved whole on 2026-09-19 (252 frames, no exclusions, the 35 amber-ringed ones approved as rendered) was imported the same day: **imported 251 · skipped 1 · failed 0**, the dry run re-run first and reproducing exactly. The skip is Chris Rabb, the politician PA-2 **reused** rather than duplicated, whose row already pointed at our CDN; the one gap is **Brandon Dukes (HD-12)**, sworn in 2026-09-08, whose portrait buckets the chamber has not generated — a dated, re-checkable blank, not an omission. PA House **202/203**, PA Senate **50/50**; statewide **275 renderable of 322 seats** by the read path's own predicate, from 4 when the stage opened. 🔴 **THE COUNT IS THE TELL, NOT THE CONTROL.** `scripts/verify-imported-headshots.py` was written wave-scoped for this: it resolves each candidate id against `COALESCE(photo_custom_url, photo_origin_url)`, refetches from the CDN and **fully decodes** — `Image.load()`, because a clean HTTP 200 can carry a truncated body — and it **asserts `--expect`**, because MN-6 shipped a verifier that printed *0 broken* while testing **none** of the 133 rows just written. Result: **control failed as required (HTTP 400), tested 252, decoded 252, broken 0, count 252 == 252**, and the count guard was watched exiting 3 on a 3-row subset before it was trusted. **217 landed at the full 600x750 and 35 at native cropped size — and 35 is exactly the amber count the operator approved, derived independently.** 🟢 **THE `.env` DOES NOT TRAVEL WITH A WORKTREE** — the import died on `KeyError: 'DATABASE_URL'` before writing anything, which is a missing file, not a bad credential. ▶ **Next: PA-5b, the two banners; the Philadelphia Sheriff's one shippable portrait; and the four debts — 7 Philadelphia licence, 13 Centre County and 8 State College publishing no portrait at all, 17 Philadelphia Council with no systematic headshot. Four different problems; do not sum them.**

Rhodes is the trap**: the SEC records him **winning the elected seat in 2022** and DNR shows him today in an **appointed** one. ▶ **The ballot says how somebody arrived; the board record says where they sit now.** One Richland elected seat is listed **Vacant** with no date, so it is written `is_vacant` with **no span** and lands in the FLAGGED half of the baseline. 🔴🔴 **A LAYER CALLED `CurrentCouncilDistricts` WAS THE SUPERSEDED MAP** — Horry's own service was last edited **2021-11-18**, three months before its ordinance 01-2022 took effect, still names **Worley, Bellamy and Vaught** (all replaced in 2022), and covers **91.26%** of the county against the loaded map's 99.99%. ▶ **A LAYER'S TITLE IS NOT ITS VINTAGE — READ ITS ATTRIBUTES**; the same org also publishes a "Staff Plan" and a "Draft", so name similarity picks a plan that was never adopted. broken detector**. 🟢 **LSA HOLDS 3418x5137 ORIGINALS AND THE EXIF PROVES IT** — the 125px file is 45 KB because 28.7 KB is leftover EXIF/Photoshop/ICC naming the source frame (Nikon Z 6_2, files at native size, do NOT request the originals.** 🔴 **The licence is a new class and was ruled shippable**: `scstatehouse.gov/disclaimer.php` makes **no copyright claim and prohibits neither reproduction nor modification**, saying only *"This information is intended for personal, not commercial, uses"* inside a warranty paragraph — PA's **Centre County** class, not Philadelphia's. 🔴🔴 **THE NO-MONOCHROME RULE WAS DOCUMENTED SINCE 2026-07-08 AND ENFORCED BY NOTHING, AND THAT COST THE ENTIRE FIRST PASS** — 156 greyscale portraits were extracted, keyed, rendered and **published for approval** before the operator caught them: *"Black and White should be a rule we have ingrained in this process."* 🟢 **It is now in the shared tooling**: `headshot_crop.py::monochrome()` measures chroma and neutral share **on the crop that ships**, the importer **refuses** a monochrome row (per-row `allow_monochrome` overrides) and the proof sheet badges and dims it — **watched failing first**, greyscale refused and colour passed in one run. ⚠ It measures **chroma, never `im.mode`**: a greyscale photo saved as RGB has three equal channels and passes any mode check. ⚠ **The manual's greyscale is the document, not the extractor** — proved twice, the PDF objects declare DeviceGray and a page rendered straight from the document measures chroma max 3. 🔴🔴 **A PIXEL IDENTITY CHECK DOES NOT TRANSFER FROM PENNSYLVANIA.** PA-5a scored MAD median **1.96** comparing `/original/` to `/300/` — the *same photograph* twice. Here the two sources are a print manual and a web downsample, and **seven metrics each overlapped their own different-person control** (raw MAD genuine max 69.14 vs control min 39.49; equalised, NCC, NCC-equalised, centre-crop and two gradient variants all likewise). The first build derived a threshold from the midpoint anyway and **rejected five members whose photos are fine**. ▶ **A METRIC THAT DOES NOT SEPARATE CANNOT BE A GATE, ONLY A RANKING** — it now badges the widest decile, and the sheet gained a `compare_url` inset so the operator makes the comparison the metric could not. 🟢 **THE MANUAL REMAINS THE BEST IDENTITY SOURCE BECAUSE IT PRINTS THE DISTRICT AND SURNAME BESIDE THE FACE** — a key the document states, never an ordinal or a position in the page's image list — and requiring the printed surname to match the roster **independently rediscovered HD-21, HD-98 and SD-12**, the three seats `sc.md` had already recorded as stale from a completely different source, plus HD-88. 🔴 **THE ASSOCIATION INVARIANT IS THE SHARED TOP EDGE AND ONLY THAT**: the bio wraps **around** the portrait, so a right-hand photo at x 189-246 has its text at x 42-185 and an x-overlap test — which looks safer — **silently rejected 41 correct pairs**. 🔴 **FILTER ON SHAPE, NOT SIZE**: a 150px "ignore the ornaments" minimum threw away real portraits (Lee Hewitt HD-108 is published at **103x130**); fixing it moved keyed coverage **120 → 163 of 170**. ⚠ **"They all look like the same person" was tested as a claim, not taken as an impression** — hashing every candidate's pixels gave **170 distinct images for 170 candidates, 0 duplicates**; uniform B&W studio portraits simply read as one face at thumbnail size. ⚠ **And that detector's first colour control was drawn FROM THE CORPUS and measured chroma 0.00**, because the first candidate is itself greyscale — **a control drawn from the data can be poisoned by the defect it is meant to detect.** ▶ **Next: SC-5b — the 54 city and county portraits, then both banners.** Full record: [`sc.md`](./sc.md).

🟡 **SC-5b APPLIED 2026-09-21 — THE CITIES AND COUNTIES HAVE PORTRAITS, 47 OF 54.** Columbia **7/7**, Myrtle Beach **7/7**, Richland **17/20**, Horry **16/21**; 47 imported, 0 skipped, 0 failed; **SC statewide 185 → 232 of 240.** Verified from outside with the count asserted: **47 tested, 47 decoded, 0 broken**, bogus-key control failed first, and **the no-monochrome gate ran over the whole wave and flagged zero** — the first wave since SC-5a made it a code rule. 🔴 **FOUR PUBLISHERS, FOUR PLATFORMS, AND ON NONE OF THEM WAS THE LINKED IMAGE THE BEST ONE.** 🟢 **THE WORDPRESS RULE PAID OUT AGAIN AND ITS SEARCH STILL MISSED HIM**: Columbia's council site is the COUNCIL's site — six member profiles at 323x427 and **no profile page for the Mayor**, whom the index links as a **440x358 LANDSCAPE** file; his real portrait is **1828x2560** on `mayor.columbiasc.gov`, and `search=Rickenmann` returns **nothing** because the file is spelled **`daniel-rikenmann`**, one k. ▶ **Search a media library for more than the correct spelling.** 🔴 **THE RESIZE CAN BE IN THE QUERY STRING** — Richland serves every councillor through `?dimension=userprofile&w=150&h=150`; **strip it and the origin is 2944x3761**, nearly 20x the linked pixels. ⚠ Not a uniform win: three origins really are ~150px, so each was measured and each member's own profile page asked too. Horry's Umbraco URLs are already originals (up to **4361x6394**). 🔴🔴 **A WAF CAN REJECT A HALF-IMPERSONATION AND ACCEPT A BARE REQUEST.** On `richlandcountysc.gov`, one image URL: **bare request 200 · Chrome UA alone 403 · UA+Referer 403 · UA+Accept 403 · full Chrome set with Referer 200.** ▶ **A BROWSER UA IS NOT A KEY — claiming to be Chrome while not sending what Chrome sends is the thing being detected**, which inverts the standing "403 with a browser UA ⇒ Playwright" rule, and the pipeline's own partial UA would have 403'd every Richland portrait. ⚠ The header set must MATCH THE RESOURCE: `Sec-Fetch-Dest: document` on a `.jpg` 403s here and alone gave four officers a false "no photo published". 🔴🔴 **THE FIRST CANDIDATE FOR SHERIFF LEON LOTT WAS THE DEPARTMENT BADGE** — 497x571, portrait-shaped, colourful, with the words "LEON LOTT" rendered inside it, so size, shape and even a name check all passed it; **looking at it is what rejected it.** ▶ **A badge is portrait-shaped — shape and size cannot tell a face from a graphic.** His real photo came only through **Playwright** (`rcsd.net` is a JS-rendered Wix build serving a plain fetch **zero** internal links), identified by the img's own `alt`, and is a **2048x1490 landscape** press shot with a second officer behind him — five crops were rendered and compared, and it ships with `anchor_x 0.28, anchor_y 0.34, zoom 1.5`. ⚠ **A FILENAME NAMING SOMEONE ELSE IS NOT ALWAYS AN ERROR**: Myrtle Beach files Councilwoman **Jackie Hatley** as `Jackie Vereen…`, and the page reads *"Jackie Vereen Hatley"* — her maiden name, the same person. Names were taken from the page text beside each image, never the filename. **Five frames were badged verify-face** for filenames that name no person (two Columbia `Untitled-design-*.png`, Horry's `sheriff-cutout.png` naming the OFFICE, Hatley, and Lott's bare Wix hash). **The 8 blanks are dated and reasoned**: Horry's **Coroner** and **Probate Judge** (the county publishes no portrait of either), **five soil-and-water commissioners** (SC DNR publishes those boards as a names-and-dates table with **no images at all**), and **one vacant** Richland soil-and-water seat. ▶ **Stage 5 now owes only the Columbia and Myrtle Beach banners.** Full record: [`sc.md`](./sc.md).

🟢 **ND-2 APPLIED 2026-09-25 — THE NORTH DAKOTA LEGISLATIVE ASSEMBLY IS SEATED.** `CC_0144` (structure) + `CC_0145` (occupancy): **141 offices — 47 Senate + 94 House — 141 seated, 0 vacant, 141 people created.** `politicians` 88,854 → 88,995 and `office_terms` 9,330 → 9,471, both **exactly +141**; `offices_missing_terms` 423 → 564 → **back to 423 / 238 unflagged**, the whole excursion accounted for. 🔴🔴 **THE ROSTER THAT LOOKS RIGHT HOLDS 148 MEMBERS — SEVEN MORE THAN THE CONSTITUTION ALLOWS.** ndlegis.gov publishes three member lists and the regular-session one is **cumulative**, so seven districts list FOUR members. **And four-in-a-district is invisible here**: North Dakota's House is legitimately two-per-district, so it reads as 2×2 and survives the shape check that would catch it instantly in a single-member state. The roster used is the **Sep 2026 special session's**, convened 2026-09-02: exactly 141. 🟢 **ALL 141 MEMBER PAGES WERE READ ANYWAY** (MN-2's rule) — 0 failures, 0 departure markers — 🔴 **and that zero was CONTROLLED**: the identical sweep over the 148-member cumulative roster found **all seven departures, every one dated to the day** (three deceased, four resigned). 🟢 **NORTH DAKOTA DATES ITS ARRIVALS, WHICH MICHIGAN AND MINNESOTA COULD NOT** — MI-2 closed with 148 undated and MN-2 with 200 unknowns; here **8 terms are day-precision** from the body's own record, and Boschee's resignation and Selzler's arrival are **consecutive days**. The mechanism is sourced, not inferred: N.D.C.C. 16.1-13-10 appoints by district party committee, confirmed on two cases. 🔴 **THE EIGHTH ARRIVAL IS ONE NO ROSTER DIFF COULD SEE** — Grindberg arrived 2024-12-01, *before* the assembly convened, so she is in all three snapshots; **a diff of snapshots only sees changes between the snapshots.** ⚠ **Two sources disagree by one day on her** (`Active December 1` vs sworn in December 2) and that is recorded, not smoothed. 🔴 **The other 133 terms are `unknown` by DECISION, not for want of a number**: both chambers serve four-year staggered terms, a re-election does not restart an occupancy, and `Senate since 1987` is a fact about the CHAMBER, not this SEAT — which was redrawn in 2021 and again in 2024. 🔴🔴 **TWO OFFICES SHARE ONE DISTRICT, SO THE DISTRICT IS NOT A KEY** — ND elects by **block voting** with no position numbers, so the pairing is made **deterministic rather than meaningful** (office by `id`, member by `full_name`, slot N to slot N) and **nothing downstream may read meaning into it**. Title carries no seat number either: production held both **Arizona's** shape (one title) and **Washington's** (Position 1/2), and ballot truth picks Arizona. 🔴 **A member's own district label is not the heading it sits under** — Finley-DeVille and Fegley both sit under "District 4" and their own rows say **4A** and **4B**. 🔴 **One name collides with a DIFFERENT person** — ND Rep. `Dick Anderson` vs the sitting **Oregon** state senator; guard lifted for exactly one row, sweep run on the guard's own key and controlled at 66 active `Johnson`s. 🔴 **The first external_id band I reserved already held 14 rows** — I had checked a *different* band and not re-checked. ✅ **EVERY GATE WAS WATCHED FAILING**, and 🔴 **two tampers that leave the total at exactly 94 are the point**: a wrong *distribution* passes any count, and in a multi-member state a wrong distribution is the likely defect. ✅ **PROBE**: Grand Forks City Hall returns **3** (1 senator + 2 representatives), **Belcourt and Fort Totten return the SAME delegation** — the court's remedy visible to a voter — and **New Town returns ONE representative**, the single-member subdistrict working end to end. 🟢 **ND-1 APPLIED 2026-09-25 — NORTH DAKOTA HAS LEGISLATIVE GEOGRAPHY FOR THE FIRST TIME.** **95 boundaries and 95 districts — 47 Senate + 48 House — 0 errors**; `districts` 10,021 → 10,116 and `geofence_boundaries` 72,205 → 72,300, both moving by **exactly 95**. `offices_missing_terms` unmoved at 423/238 unflagged. 🔴🔴 **THE HOUSE IS MULTI-MEMBER AND THE POLYGON COUNT IS NOT THE SEAT COUNT — a program first.** N.D. Const. Art. IV § 2 gives every district one senator and TWO representatives, so **48 House polygons carry 94 seats**: 46 districts electing two each, plus single-member subdistricts **4A and 4B** electing one each. ▶ **Every gate that reads “one office per district” is wrong here, including MI-4's per-district control, and the program's four-answer probe returns FIVE answers in North Dakota.** 🔴 **VINTAGE PROVED, AND NORTH DAKOTA GIVES EVIDENCE OHIO COULD NOT.** The operative map is the one the U.S. District Court ordered on **2024-01-08** in *Turtle Mountain Band of Chippewa Indians v. Howe*; the remedy **dissolved subdistricts 9A/9B into a whole District 9** and left 4A/4B standing, so the code set itself dates the map — TIGER 2022 and 2023 carry 49 House polygons with 09A/09B, TIGER 2024 and 2025 carry 48 without them. ⚠ **BUT THE CODE SET IS NOT THE PROOF**: the same order redrew **District 15, which kept its number**, and a code-set check is blind to that. The proof is geometric — all 95 polygons were located at their own published internal points inside the state's own `NDGISHUB Legislative Districts` layer and agree **48/48 House and 47/47 Senate, 0 ambiguous**. **The control failed as required**: TIGER 2022 disagrees on House 9A→9 and 9B→15 and Senate 9→15. 🔴 **AND IT STILL AGREES ON 47 OF 49 AND 46 OF 47** — two districts out of forty-seven moved, so a count, a shape check, a spot check **and even a 96%-agreement threshold all pass the STRUCK-DOWN map.** 🔴 **THE SUPERSEDED PLAN IS PUBLISHED BESIDE THE LIVE ONE AND SORTS ABOVE IT ON ONE FIELD** — `NDGISHUB 2021 67th Assembly Legislative Districts` is HB 1504, `modified` 2021-11-12 but **`issued` 2025-03-24**, three years later than the live layer's 2022-01-21. Name the layer; never take the newest. 🟢 Unlike Ohio's Secretary of State there is **no WAF** — ndgishub.nd.gov answers a bare request with HTTP 200. 🔴 **A ND HOUSE DISTRICT IS NOT AN INTEGER**: `3804A`/`3804B` wrote **48 rows with 48 distinct `ocd_id`s** (`sldl:4A`, `sldl:4B`), the MN `08A`/MD `1A` collapse held off by `ocdDistrictSuffix`. 🔴 **AND THE SAME TRAP BIT MY OWN VERIFY QUERY — THE COUNT WAS THE TELL**: a join stripping the letter turned `3804A` into `04` against a Senate `004`, silently dropped both subdistricts, and reported a confident **46** under a header saying 48. ✅ **Gates**: House nests in its Senate district **48 of 48 by `ST_Covers`**; **4A ∪ 4B = Senate 4 exactly** (1,582.6207 + 2,666.3512 = 4,248.9719 sq mi, overlap 0.000000, symmetric difference 0.000000); per-district control **47/47 and 48/48 resolve to exactly themselves**, controlled by the same sweep returning **0** against Minnesota and **23 not 48** when translated 3° east. **All three new pre-flight assertions were watched failing first** — and 🔴 **the count assertion HIDES the subdistrict assertion on the real struck-down file**, so the expected count had to be lifted deliberately to test the inner branch. **When two gates guard the same file, the outer one prevents you from testing the inner one.** `check:reachability` green, nothing regressed. ▶ **Stage 2 owes 141 offices — 47 Senate + 94 House — and ND currently has ZERO state legislative offices.** Detail: [`nd.md`](./nd.md).

Re-measure rather than trust these once any wave has applied. 🔴 **The two tables below had NOT been re-measured since 2026-08-28. Four of sixteen rows were wrong in the first and TEN of sixteen in the second**, and in both directions: SC read `0/124` with its General Assembly fully seated, PA read `0 | 0 | 0` geofence polygons with 253 loaded, six states read `place 0` while every one of them already held a `place` layer — and FL and CA were recorded complete after six of their seats had gone vacant. ▶ **A stale baseline is worse than no baseline — it is read as a measurement.** The tables and the arithmetic line under the first one are now generated from production rather than maintained by hand.

`photo_custom_url` — the field that actually renders — pointing at **somebody else's server**.
The old table had no such column, so such a row would count as "hosted" and read as done.

corpus renders from a third party's host through `photo_custom_url`. The column stays because the
distinction is worth watching, not because it currently finds anything.

🔴🔴 **THE FIRST RUN OF THIS COLUMN REPORTED 407 OF OUR OWN PORTRAITS AS SOMEBODY
ELSE'S — CA 98, IN 18, MD 187, UT 103, TX 1.** Our bucket has **two equivalent public URL forms**:
`https://<ref>.storage.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/politician_photos/...` and
`https://<ref>.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/politician_photos/...`. The predicate matched
the hostname spelling of the first, so every row written in the second form read as external. All
407 were fetched and confirmed **HTTP 200, real JPEG, from this bucket**.
▶ **Key on the PROJECT REF and the BUCKET, never on the hostname spelling.** It is
[[feedback_predicate_shape_not_value]] again: the predicate read the shape of the value instead of
what the value is. ⚠ And it is the reason the column is kept rather than deleted — a detector
that has been caught lying once and fixed is worth more than one that was never tested.

✅ **CONTROL: ALL FIFTEEN ROWS OF THE PREVIOUS TABLE REPRODUCE EXACTLY**, including the ones that
were not round numbers — Baldwin 11/11/**7**, Manatee 12/**11**/11, Columbus 16/16/**15**. A query
that cannot reproduce the known rows cannot be trusted on the unknown ones. 🔴 **The other 23
governments were sitting under `every other jurisdiction | 0 | 0 | 0`**, which is what a row like
that always eventually means: nobody re-read it.

**The stage-5 debt is 78, and four jurisdictions are most of it:** Philadelphia **24**, Centre County
**13**, Lake County **10**, State College **8**. Philadelphia is the recorded licence debt; Centre and
State College **publish no portrait at all**, which is a different problem and does not get better by
trying again.

🔴🔴 **LOS ANGELES COUNTY'S GOVERNMENT ROW IS SHARED WITH ANOTHER PROGRAMME AND THE
RAW COUNT IS 481, NOT 8.** 473 Superior Court judgeships hang off the same
`Los Angeles County, California, US` row as the 5 Supervisors and 3 countywide officials this table
is about. Counted raw it reads **481 offices / 416 seated / 11 headshots** and buries the 8. The
generator excludes the bulk bench chambers by name. ⚠ **It is NOT a blanket "no judges" rule** —
Gary's City Court is ONE seat and IS a Knight office (Gary elects a judge and Fort Wayne does not),
and county Probate Judges are elected county officers.

⚠ **ONE GOVERNMENT ROW CARRIES NO STATE SUFFIX.** Every other row here is `<name>, <State>, US`;
San José's is the bare string **`City of San Jose`**. A state-scoped match drops it **silently**, and
did on the first run of this query — the row simply was not there, with no error. It is listed by
exact name rather than by loosening the state scope, because that scope is what keeps
`Summit County, Utah`, `City of Saint Paul, Texas` and `City of Boulder City, Nevada` out. The
generator asserts all four decoys are excluded and says so on every run.

### Earlier readings, kept for the record

Bradenton and Manatee measured 2026-08-28 after FL-3. Manatee's twelfth office is Commission
District 1, flagged vacant since 2026-02-24 — the incumbent died and the Governor left the seat empty,
so it is on the 2026 ballot for a two-year unexpired term. **17 people, 0 headshots: that is the whole
of FL-3's stage-5 debt so far.**

Re-measured 2026-08-30 after FL-6, which closed stages 3 and 4. **Florida now holds 73 local and
county offices across SEVEN governments, 72 seated, 1 vacant** — plus 164 legislative offices. Palm
Beach has **no city half**, so it is a county row with no municipal partner. **All 72 now carry a headshot (FL-7, 2026-08-30) — the slice's stage-5 debt is CLEARED.**

Re-measured 2026-09-02 after GA-6, which closed the slice. **Georgia holds 49 local and county
offices across THREE governments, 49 seated, 0 vacant** — Milledgeville 7/7 + Baldwin County 11/11,
Columbus Consolidated 16/16, Macon-Bibb 15/15 — plus 240 legislative and statewide offices, 239
seated. **44 of the 49 carry a headshot; 5 do not** — Baldwin's four, plus the Muscogee Probate Judge.
The Bibb Coroner was closed by GA-7 from a Macon Magazine portrait, using a per-person crop override
to exclude an advocacy sign the default crop included. **The legislature is 239 of 239.**

The 2026-09-01 reading below stands as written, for the record. 🔴 **14 of 18 now carry a headshot (2026-09-01); 4 do not** — measured with the
`HAS_RENDERABLE_PHOTO_SQL` predicate from `photoCoverage.ts` rather than by counting
`politician_images` rows, because a `politician_images` row changes nothing a voter sees.
The four without one are the **Probate Judge, Tax Commissioner, Coroner and Surveyor** — part-time
county offices with no portrait on the county site, no Ballotpedia photo (their pages are stubs) and
no county Democratic committee site in existence. 🔴 All four carry `photo_custom_url` **and**
`photo_origin_url` NULL: a blank beats a link, because a page URL counts as coverage under
`HAS_RENDERABLE_PHOTO_SQL` and would hide the person behind a broken image.

⚠ **Baldwin is a FOURTH county convention in five counties**: 5 single-member seats, **no at-large
member**, and the Chair elected by the Board from among the five. Against Manatee 5+2, Leon 5+2, Palm
Beach 7 single-member and Miami-Dade 13 + a separate Mayor. **No county convention has repeated yet.**

Miami-Dade is the largest single jurisdiction in the program so far at 19 offices — 13 single-member
commission districts, a countywide Mayor who is not a Board member, and 5 constitutional officers.
⚠ Its **~60 Community Council seats are deliberately unmodelled**, and are the largest single block of
elected local offices found anywhere in the program.

### Banners present

`long beach`, `san jose`, and — from FL-7 — `miami`, `tallahassee`, `bradenton` plus the program's
first COUNTY key, `12099` Palm Beach, and — from GA-3/GA-4/GA-5 — **`milledgeville`, `columbus` and
`macon`**, which complete Georgia. **17 cities still missing.**

✅ **MILLEDGEVILLE'S BANNER IS LIVE (GA-3, 2026-09-01)**: the Old State Capitol / Georgia Military
College across State House Square — Clifflandis, **CC0** — certified in BOTH boxes and registered
`match:'exact'` in [essentials#112](https://github.com/EmpoweredVote/essentials/pull/112).

🔴🔴 **A FRONTAL BUILDING PORTRAIT IS NOT A BANNER SUBJECT — THE BEND/FL-7 FAILURE IN A THIRD DRESS.**
Milledgeville's signature building is the 1839 Old Governor's Mansion, and it FAILS the desktop box:
from a ~1.42:1 source the 3.148:1 crop drops 55% of the rows and the band keeps 52.5% of the rest,
about a quarter of the original. Swept at anchors 0.30/0.45/0.60 across TWO photographs, every result
is a wall of windows. ▶ **Prefer a horizontally arranged subject, and a source already near 3:1.**

🔴🔴 **TEST COLOUR, NOT ONLY LICENCE AND RATIO.** The two widest and most permissively licensed sources
found — HABS federal work, public domain, 1.62:1, which would have survived the band better than
anything else — measured **100% GREYSCALE**. No licence or aspect check catches that.

🟢 **§8.1 ADJACENCY CLEARED FOR ALL THREE GEORGIA CITIES, measured 2026-09-01.** `states/GA.jpg` is
**the Atlanta skyline** (Midtown, 1700x540, 3.148:1). Atlanta is in none of this slice's three
jurisdictions, so **no state-banner move is needed** for Milledgeville, Columbus or Macon — unlike
Miami, where the state banner WAS the city's own skyline, and unlike Wichita, Detroit and Charlotte,
which still have that conflict ahead of them.

⚠ **No `CURATED_LOCAL` key is scoped `GA` yet** — Milledgeville would be the program's first Georgia
city banner. The registry is `src/lib/buildingImages.js` in the **essentials** repo (branch `main`),
not this one, so the banner ships as a separate PR there.
⚠ **`states/GA-v2.jpg` returns HTTP 400, not 404** — the missing-object signature, re-confirmed. ⚠ **Baldwin County needs
no county key**: unlike Palm Beach it has a city half, so the city banner covers the jurisdiction.
Palm Beach took its own COUNTY key only because it had no municipal partner.

🔴 **Miami's §8.1 conflict was resolved by MOVING the state banner, not by finding a different city
photograph.** Florida's state banner WAS Miami's skyline; it is now `cities/miami.jpg`, and the state
took `states/FL-v2.jpg` (Rookery Bay). ▶ **The same move is available to Wichita, Detroit and
Charlotte** — the remaining three with the same conflict. Any replacement state banner must be
VERSIONED, never an overwrite: the CDN does not reliably purge.
